coordinates,left+,right-. Define F(x)=log(2^(-m)sum_leaves exp(<a_s,x>)).

# ONE independent analytical search: the specified tree-Gibbs terminal entropy

You are max4,gpt-6-astra/max;consult OFF. Actual previous completed48,
attempted49 ended at cap. NEW ONE round,completed ceiling49/attempted50.
ZERO mathematical evaluator,numerical/solver,DP,replay,machine-algebra,
installation,formal/fact_submit,consult or native-subagent allowance.

This is independent proof search on a SPECIFIED mathematical object,
not a premature audit or acceptance of an unfinished target proof.
Original all-n/all-h exact-profile Goal and W=2R remain unchanged.
Even success for the diagnostic H_m sequence would leave its separate
uniform arbitrary-word bridge. All new claims below are UNVERIFIED.

Concrete shared input7ec66d4b933755c3,public mirror
research/shared_evidence/7ec66d4b933755c3.md,
SHA25633ee208e5bf353343d9f55ccafe5e6007c2e80208bb70cced766176a713bf194.
max is developing the full actual-function draft. You may read its
new shared updates,never its private research files. Reproduce every
preliminary identity you need from the definitions below;no unverified
source statement is a correctness premise. If a stated preliminary
fails,report the exact error before using it.

## Fixed object, no fresh-child or online assumption

For the original enclosing m define
beta_m=1-1/m,c_m=(1+sqrt2)(1+1/m),a_m=beta_m/c_m,
H0=(),H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_m*c_m^j,H_j),j<m,n=2^m-1.
Every child retains the SAME enclosing m.
Put r=2/c_m,Z_m=sum_(j=0)^(m-1)r^j,w_j=r^j/Z_m.
On the full depth-m binary tree use one orthonormal coordinate for
each internal node. A leaf feature a_s is the signed root-to-leaf
sum of sqrt(w_j) times the level-j coordinates. Use left branch +,
right branch -,consistently with the all-left spine below.

The static function is
F_m(x)=log(2^(-m) sum_leaves exp(<a_s,x>)).
The reported unique minimum is0 at x=0 and the reported sharp global
gradient Lipschitz constant is Ltree=(1+w_0)/2. These assertions must
be established before using the normalization. The initial point has
only all-left-spine coordinates,with sqrt(w_j)*x_(0^j)=log2.
The reported exact squared radius is
R_m^2=(log2)^2*r^(1-m)*Z_m^2.

Run ACTUAL GD with physical steps H_m divided by Ltree. The spine is
reported invariant by symmetry. Let y_i,j=sqrt(w_j)*x_i,(0^j).
Group probabilities into the m first-exit side groups j=0,...,m-1
and the final all-left leaf j=m. Initially each side group has mass
1/(m+4),and the final leaf has mass4/(m+4). The reported exact update is

y_(i+1),j = y_i,j -(h_i*w_j/Ltree)
                         *(sum_(k>j) P_i,k-P_i,j).

The P_i,k must be the probabilities of this SAME finite static Gibbs
function,not free choices. Derive their exact dependence on y and keep
their changing subtree masses. A child's gradient is scaled by its
current mass;it does NOT follow a fresh copy of H_j's isolated law.

## Precisely selected mathematical question

Investigate whether for every sufficiently large m the ACTUAL terminal
entropy/function value satisfies

F_m(x_n) >= Ltree*R_m^2/(2 B_n),  n=2^m-1,

where B_n is the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark with C0=B0=1,
C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)(C_k+C_l+sqrt(C_k^2+6C_k C_l+C_l^2))/2,
B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)[2C_k+B_l/2+sqrt(B_l^2+8C_k B_l)/2].
Do not replace this exact threshold by an unproved decimal constant,
an exponent-only inequality,or an asymptotic recurrence with missing
uniform control. A quantitative different bound is useful only with
its actual implication and remaining gap stated exactly.

Use probability/entropy methods,changes of measure or tree/path
combinatorics if useful,but derive the actual state/payment inequality.
No assumed concentration,independent-subtree reset or missing normalizing
factor. The target here is the terminal entropy at a fully specified
trajectory,not a generic lemma equivalent to the entire original Goal.
If it is false,seek a rigorous asymptotic obstruction for THIS object.
If it remains unresolved,publish the strongest proved invariant and
the precise remaining inequality,with no inflated completion claim.

Useful accepted inputs:1252b8823bb5a290 for interpolation conventions,
b98bb807d514e80d/1fca3e5dd555cb4a/dfde2fa326c908bf for scope-correct
complete references,and04219554e41d9750 for the exact benchmark facts
it actually proves. No new benchmark DP/evaluation is authorized.

Publish COMPLETE evidence through shared global memory. ONE round
